Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the extraction structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 433. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 166 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_433(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 433. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^166(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
